rule (modulo E) Verifier_Challenge:
   [ Fr( ~n ) ] --> [ VChal( ~n ), Out( ~n ) ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) Holder_Present:
   [ !Sk( 'a10_kbjwt', t, k ), In( n ) ]
  --[ Presented( n ) ]->
   [ Out( <<'kb', n>, sign(<'kb', n>, k)> ) ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) Accept_Presentation_main:
   [ !Acc( 'a10_kbjwt', pkD, 'main' ), VChal( n ), In( <<'kb', n>, s> ) ]
  --[ Eq( verify(s, <'kb', n>, pkD), true ), AcceptPres( n ) ]->
   [ ]

  /*
  rule (modulo AC) Accept_Presentation_main:
     [ !Acc( 'a10_kbjwt', pkD, 'main' ), VChal( n ), In( <<'kb', n>, s> ) ]
    --[ Eq( z, true ), AcceptPres( n ) ]->
     [ ]
    variants (modulo AC)
    1. n     = n.11
       pkD   = pkD.12
       s     = s.13
       z     = verify(s.13, <'kb', n.11>, pkD.12)
    
    2. n     = n.24
       pkD   = pk(x.46)
       s     = sign(<'kb', n.24>, x.46)
       z     = true
  */

   [
   !Acc( 'a10_kbjwt', pkD, 'alt' ),
   !ExpVal( 'a02_lote_pid', 'a10_kbjwt', 'none' ), VChal( n ),
   In( <<'kb', n>, s> )

lemma executable:
  exists-trace "∃ n #p #j. (Presented( n ) @ #p) ∧ (AcceptPres( n ) @ #j)"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all satisfying traces:
"∃ n #p #j. (Presented( n ) @ #p) ∧ (AcceptPres( n ) @ #j)"
*/
by sorry

lemma G2_presentation_unforgeability:
  all-traces
  "∀ n #j.
    (AcceptPres( n ) @ #j) ⇒ (∃ #p. (Presented( n ) @ #p) ∧ (#p < #j))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ n #j.
  (AcceptPres( n ) @ #j) ∧ ∀ #p. (Presented( n ) @ #p) ⇒ ¬(#p < #j)"
*/
simplify
solve( AcceptPres( n ) @ #j )
  case Accept_Presentation_alt
  solve( !Acc( 'a10_kbjwt', pk(x), 'alt' ) ▶₀ #j )
    case AccIntro_a07_kimlik__a10_kbjwt_alt_case_001
    solve( !ExpVal( 'a02_lote_pid', 'a10_kbjwt', 'none' ) ▶₁ #j )
      case ExpAcc_a02_lote_pid__a10_kbjwt_main
      solve( VChal( n ) ▶₂ #j )
        case Verifier_Challenge
        solve( !KU( sign(<'kb', ~n>, x) ) @ #vk.4 )
          case Holder_Present_case_1
          by contradiction /* from formulas */
        next
          case Holder_Present_case_2
          by contradiction /* from formulas */
        next
          case c_sign
          solve( !KU( sign(<'intro', 'a02_lote_pid', 'a03_ca', pk(x.4), 'main'>,
                           ~km)
                 ) @ #vk.69 )
            case Intro_a02_lote_pid__a03_ca
            solve( !KU( sign(<'intro', 'a03_ca', 'a04_ihr', pk(x.2), 'main'>, ~km)
                   ) @ #vk.65 )
              case Intro_a03_ca__a04_ihr
              solve( !KU( sign(<'intro', 'a04_ihr', 'a07_kimlik', pk(x.1), 'alt'>,
                               ~km.2)
                     ) @ #vk.48 )
                case Intro_a04_ihr__a07_kimlik
                solve( !KU( sign(<'intro', 'a07_kimlik', 'a10_kbjwt', pk(x), 'alt'>, ~ka)
                       ) @ #vk.41 )
                  case Intro_a07_kimlik__a10_kbjwt
                  solve( !KU( ~ka ) @ #vk.75 )
                    case Break_a10_kbjwt_alt
                    solve( !KU( sign(<'intro', 'a02_lote_pid', 'a03_ca', pk(x.2), 'main'>,
                                     ~km.1)
                           ) @ #vk.74 )
                      case Intro_a02_lote_pid__a03_ca
                      solve( !KU( sign(<'intro', 'a03_ca', 'a04_ihr', pk(x.1), 'main'>, ~km)
                             ) @ #vk.67 )
                        case Intro_a03_ca__a04_ihr
                        solve( !KU( sign(<'intro', 'a04_ihr', 'a05_meta', pk(x), 'main'>, ~km.2)
                               ) @ #vk.58 )
                          case Intro_a04_ihr__a05_meta
                          solve( !KU( sign(<'exp', 'a05_meta', 'a07_kimlik', 'none'>, ~km.3)
                                 ) @ #vk.49 )
                            case c_sign
                            by solve( !KU( ~km.3 ) @ #vk.62 )
                          qed
                        next
                          case c_sign
                          by solve( !KU( ~km.2 ) @ #vk.62 )
                        qed
                      next
                        case c_sign
                        by solve( !KU( ~km ) @ #vk.69 )
                      qed
                    next
                      case c_sign
                      by solve( !KU( ~km.1 ) @ #vk.76 )
                    qed
                  qed
                next
                  case c_sign
                  solve( !KU( ~ka ) @ #vk.76 )
                    case Break_a07_kimlik_alt
                    solve( !KU( sign(<'intro', 'a02_lote_pid', 'a03_ca', pk(x.3), 'main'>,
                                     ~km.1)
                           ) @ #vk.74 )
                      case Intro_a02_lote_pid__a03_ca
                      solve( !KU( sign(<'intro', 'a03_ca', 'a04_ihr', pk(x.2), 'main'>, ~km)
                             ) @ #vk.67 )
                        case Intro_a03_ca__a04_ihr
                        solve( !KU( sign(<'intro', 'a04_ihr', 'a05_meta', pk(x.1), 'main'>,
                                         ~km.2)
                               ) @ #vk.58 )
                          case Intro_a04_ihr__a05_meta
                          solve( !KU( sign(<'exp', 'a05_meta', 'a07_kimlik', 'none'>, ~km.3)
                                 ) @ #vk.49 )
                            case c_sign
                            by solve( !KU( ~km.3 ) @ #vk.63 )
                          qed
                        next
                          case c_sign
                          by solve( !KU( ~km.2 ) @ #vk.63 )
                        qed
                      next
                        case c_sign
                        by solve( !KU( ~km ) @ #vk.70 )
                      qed
                    next
                      case c_sign
                      by solve( !KU( ~km.1 ) @ #vk.77 )
                    qed
                  qed
                qed
              next
                case c_sign
                by solve( !KU( ~km.2 ) @ #vk.76 )
              qed
            next
              case c_sign
              by solve( !KU( ~km ) @ #vk.76 )
            qed
          next
            case c_sign
            by solve( !KU( ~km ) @ #vk.76 )
          qed
        qed
      qed
    qed
  next
    case AccIntro_a07_kimlik__a10_kbjwt_alt_case_002
    solve( !ExpVal( 'a02_lote_pid', 'a10_kbjwt', 'none' ) ▶₁ #j )
      case ExpAcc_a02_lote_pid__a10_kbjwt_main
      solve( VChal( n ) ▶₂ #j )
        case Verifier_Challenge
        solve( !KU( sign(<'kb', ~n>, x) ) @ #vk.4 )
          case Holder_Present_case_1
          by contradiction /* from formulas */
        next
          case Holder_Present_case_2
          by contradiction /* from formulas */
        next
          case c_sign
          solve( !KU( sign(<'intro', 'a02_lote_pid', 'a03_ca', pk(x.4), 'main'>,
                           ~km)
                 ) @ #vk.74 )
            case Intro_a02_lote_pid__a03_ca
            solve( !KU( sign(<'intro', 'a03_ca', 'a04_ihr', pk(x.2), 'main'>, ~km)
                   ) @ #vk.70 )
              case Intro_a03_ca__a04_ihr
              solve( !KU( sign(<'intro', 'a04_ihr', 'a07_kimlik', pk(x.1), 'alt'>,
                               ~km.2)
                     ) @ #vk.50 )
                case Intro_a04_ihr__a07_kimlik
                solve( !KU( sign(<'intro', 'a07_kimlik', 'a10_kbjwt', pk(x), 'alt'>, ~ka)
